We can design complex/real polynomials modulo x^m-1 with flat square coefficients. This is exactly finding nonnegative coefficients polynomial P mod x^m-1 such that P^2 has coefficients constant (or nearly). This is a "perfect difference set" or "Bose-Chowla" Sidon set if indicators. For weights, could use Fourier: need P(roots)^2 has flat DFT coefficients? Wait cyclic square coefficients c_k. Constant c_k =1/m if sum=1. Then P(z)^2 =1/m ∑ z^k =1/m(1-z^m)/(1-z) on z roots. At m-th roots ω≠1, P(ω)^2 =0. So P must have zeros at all nontrivial m-th roots, meaning P(z) = c (1+...+z^{m-1}), i.e. uniform, whose square has max m not constant. Thus exact constant impossible if sum=1 and cyclic length m? Let's check: A length m polynomial with cyclic convolution square c_k. DFT: C_j= P(ω_j)^2. If C_j constant 1/m for all j, then P(ω_j)=±1/√m; for j=0 P(1)=1 => 1/√m impossible unless m=1. So average coefficient 1/m impossible due to DC: C_0=P(1)^2=1, so cyclic max≥1. Score then ≥2, not helpful. But linear convolution has αverage 1/(2n-1) because no circular alias and degree 2n-2 length bigger. So cyclic not directly.

But if we take length m sequence with small linear max, average=1/(2m-1) ~1/(2m); cyclic DC constraint not relevant because linear degree 2n-2 has average ~1/(2n). We can think of P of length n as maybe half of cyclic length 2n? If sequence placed in first half of length N=2n maybe cyclic? Hmm.

Let's design `optimize_flow`:
```
def optimize_flow(a, time_budget, rng, params):
    theta = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    theta -= theta.mean()
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = score(a)
    lr = 0.05
    beta1,beta2=0.9,0.999
    m=v=zeros
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time< budget:
       a=softmax(theta)
       b=full_conv(a); maxb= b.max(); cur=sc
       if cur < best: update
       tau = maxb * temperature_scale (e.g. 0.02? Actually tau = maxb * 0.05?)
       w = multi_softmax(b, tau)
       grad_a=2*hankel_grad(a,w)
       # center grad to keep softmax scale? c = dot(a,grad_a)? For flow, subtract mean? To preserve sum without exact normalize, use grad_theta = grad_a - dot? Actually gradient wrt θ = a*(grad_a - dot). Multiplicative step θ -= η gradθ. Equivalent a *= exp(-η grad_a + η dot). To keep sum, η dot adjusts global. So use grad_a directly with centering: a_new = a * exp(-η (grad_a - grad_central?)). We can compute grad_theta/a = grad_a - dot; use theta update with grad_theta.
       Adam: m=... grad_theta
       delta = - (m_hat/sqrt(v_hat+eps)) maybe scaled. theta += eta*delta
       a_try=softmax(theta); score_try. If better than cur: accept and mean subtract. else maybe revert and reduce eta.
       # Alternatively directly update a = a*exp(-eta*(grad_a - np.mean(grad_a?))) and normalize. Simpler no theta.
```

Polish exact local search:
Given a normalized, objective F=max b. We can perform "active set transfer" moves:
- Let active_lags = indices where b close to max (within ε).
- For a random pair of coordinates (r,c), move δ from r to c preserving sum: a' = a; a[r]-=δ, a[c]+=δ, clip. Try small δ around optimum. This changes b_k linearly: Δb_k = δ(a[c]-a[r]) for k where? Specifically coefficient b_{i+j}: if moving mass from r to c, b_k changes accordingly. Could find δ minimizing max by 1D. But evaluating full each time okay. We can do random `shift` moves: choose source i and target j, amount t = s * a[i] (e.g. s=0.01-0.1), clip at 0.1 a[i]. Evaluate; accept if improves (or simulated anneal). This is simple and can polish. For n=128, many moves. Use multiple scales. Previous random polish with full normal perturbations maybe too disruptive.

Coordinate move with two coordinates might be more efficient. We can implement `coordinate_polish(a, time_budget, rng)`:
   best=a, b, score.
   scale=0.05
   while time: for many iterations:
      pick i,j random; t = scale * a[i] * rng sign? Since move from high to low? We can choose source i,j and amount t = u * a[source] with u∈[0.01,0.1] times random sign? But move can be in two directions. Simpler: choose pair, propose new_a = a; new_a[i]*= (1-α), new_a[j]*=(1+α*ratio?) preserving roughly. Then normalize.
      evaluate. If improves, accept; else if random accept.
   Decrease scale.
But high-dimensional random walk slow. However as polish after gradient may help.

def optimize_theta(a0, time_budget, rng, topK=16, use_adam=True, lr0=0.05, betas=(0.9,0.99)):
    a = normalize(a0)
    theta = np.log(a+1e-300) - np.log(a+1e-300).mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); cur_sc=best_sc
    lr=lr0
    no_improve=0
    last=time
    while time remaining:
       a=softmax(theta)
       b=full_conv(a); maxb=b.max(); cur=2*n*maxb
       if cur<best_sc: update; no_improve=0
       else no_improve+=1
       # gradient
       if no_improve > 1000:
           # reset around best
           theta = np.log(best_a + 1e-12); theta -= mean
           m=v=0; lr=lr0*(1+self rng); no_improve=0; t=0; continue
       w=weights
       grad_a=2*grad_hankel(a,w)
       # optional: if grad_a all zero? no.
       dot = np.dot(a, grad_a)
       g = a * (grad_a - dot)  # gradient in full theta
       # optionally set mean zero: g -= g.mean()
       g -= g.mean()  # remove null direction; good.
       if use_adam:
          t+=1
          m=...; v=...
          step = (m_hat)/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
       else:
          m = beta1*m+(1-beta1)*g? Use velocity
       # line search
       tried factors [1,0.5,0.25,0.125]
       for fac:
           theta_try = theta + lr*fac*step (step direction maybe -g; set step = -m_hat? careful)
           if use momentum, step already direction (negative). So theta_try=theta + lr*fac*dir
           a_try=softmax(theta_try)
           sc=2*n*max(full_conv(a_try))
           if sc <= cur*1.0000? accept
       if not accept and random<0.02: accept worst? else keep.
       # adapt lr: if accepted good decrease no_improve; if no improve for many, lr*=0.8
```

def make_weights(b, K, C, rng=None):
   m=len(b)
   if m <= K: uniform
   # top K exactly and maybe LSE broad
   top = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
   w = np.zeros_like(b); w[top]=1.0
   w /= w.sum()
   # could blend with softmax broader to include others
   maxb=b.max()
   tau=maxb*0.05? beta=1/tau
   sw = softmax(beta*(b-maxb))
   w = (1-alpha)*w + alpha*sw
```

```
def make_weights(b, K=32, alpha=0.3, C=10, flat_frac=0.1):
   maxb=b.max()
   active = b >= maxb*(1-flat_frac)
   if active.sum() < K:
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       active_idx=idx
   else: active_idx=np.where(active)[0]
   w = np.zeros; w[active]=1
   # blend with normalized softmax of all
   sw=softmax((b-maxb)*C/maxb) if maxb>0 else uniform
   w = (1-alpha)*w/w.sum() + alpha*sw
```

Optimize theta details more.

Let's write functions:
- `softmax(theta)`:
```
def softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    theta -= np.max(theta)
    z=np.exp(theta)
    s=z.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s==0: z=np.exp(theta - np.min(theta)); s=z.sum()
    return z/s
```
But if theta has large range >700, subtract max. Good.

- `normalize_nonnegative(a)`:
```
a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: ones; return a/s
```

- `score_norm(a,b=None)`:
```
if b is None: b=full_conv(a)
return 2*a.size*float(np.max(b))
```
Assume sum 1. But due softmax, sum 1.

- `full_conv(a)`: Use FFT. For small n maybe use np.convolve? Implement:
```
def full_conv(x):
    n=x.size
    if n<64:
       return np.convolve(x,x)
    size=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()  # because 2n-1 length, need size >=2n-1. (2n-2).bit_length? For length L=2n-1, p2 = 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
```
Check: n=128, L=255, L-1=254, bit_length=8, size=256? But need size >=255; 256 yes. n=2, L=3, L-1=2 bit_length=2 size=4. OK.
Use rfft: fa=np.fft.rfft(x,fsize); y=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,fsize)[:L]
```
Clip: small negative due FFT; use y=np.maximum(y,0)? But max could be slightly negative if all zero; but a positive. For score, max of max(0,y) okay. But for gradient weights, b should nonnegative. Use `y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)` maybe. But if all small negative? no. Clipping improves.
```
```
For gradient hankel with y length L2=3n-2:
```
def hankel_grad(a,w):
   n=a.size
   L=3*n-2
   fsize=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
   ar=a[::-1]
   fa=rfft(ar,fsize); fw=rfft(w,fsize)
   c=irfft(fa*fw,fsize)[:L]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Multi-scale top weights:
```
def make_weights(b, K, mix_broad=0.25, C=10.0):
    m=b.size
    maxb = float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 0: uniform return
    # top K indices
    if K>=m: idx=np.arange(m)
    else: idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    # active within flat_frac
    flat = np.where(b >= maxb*(1-0.05))[0]
    if flat.size:
       # union without set? Use boolean mask
       mask=np.zeros(m,dtype=bool); mask[idx]=True; mask[flat]=True
    else: mask[idx]=True
    w=mask.astype(float)
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws==0: uniform
    else: w/=ws
    # broad softmax
    tau=maxb/ C  # C=10 -> exponent (b-maxb)/tau = -C*(maxb-b)/maxb, top weights e^0, others less
    shift=(b-maxb)/tau
    shift-=shift.max() # top 0
    sw=np.exp(shift)
    ss=sw.sum()
    if ss<1e-300: sw = mask? ; else sw/=ss
    w=(1-mix_broad)*w + mix_broad*sw
    w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
If topK very small, broad includes others.
Maybe C=10; active threshold 0.95. For flat plateau, active mask size ~n/2; mask weights 1 each, broad less; okay.

Could choose K adapt to n: `K = max(8, min(64, n//8?))`. For n=128, K=16; n=2048 K=64. Active adds. Good.

Optimization function:

def optimize_theta(a0, time_budget, rng, K=None, lr0=0.1, topK=None, restart=3000, noise=0.1):
   n=a0.size
   a=normalize(a0)
   theta=np.log(a+1e-300); theta-=theta.mean()
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(a); cur_sc=best_sc
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   lr=lr0; beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
   last_improve=0; no_improve=0; stall=0
   it=0
   t0=time
   while time<t0+time_budget:
      b=full_conv(a); maxb=b.max(); cur=2*n*maxb
      if cur < best_sc*(1-1e-12):
          best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it; no_improve=0
      else: no_improve+=1
      if no_improve>restart:
          # reset
          base=best_a
          # add noise in theta
          theta(np.log(base+1e-12)); theta-=mean; theta += noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
          m=v=0; t=0; lr=lr0*(0.8+0.6*rng.random()); no_improve=0; last_improve=it
          continue
      w=make_weights(b,K,K? mix maybe)
      ga=2.0*hankel_grad(a,w)
      dot=np.dot(a,ga)
      g=a*(ga-dot)
      g -= g.mean() # remove null
      # If gradient norm tiny, perturb
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break/reset
      gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gnorm < 1e-15:
          # add tiny random
          g += 1e-8*rng.standard_normal(n); g-=g.mean()
      # Adam update
      t+=1
      m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*g
      v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*g*g
      mh=m/(1-beta1**t); vh=v/(1-beta2**t)
      step=mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
      # line search
      tried=False
      for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
          theta_try=theta - lr*fac*step
          theta_try -= theta_try.mean() # to control drift? But subtract mean changes objective invariant; safe but gradient? It applies same to all, but due floating, okay. However if we subtract mean each line search, direction with zero mean? g mean zero already, step mean not necessarily? We can do.
          a_try=softmax(theta_try)
          # maybe enforce small floor? no
          b_try=full_conv(a_try); sc_try=2*n*float(b_try.max())
          if sc_try <= cur*(1+1e-12) or rng.random()<0.02: # accept
              theta=theta_try; a=a_try; cur=sc_try
              if sc_try < best_sc: update best? (already maybe in next loop); no_improve=0; tried=True
              break
      if not tried:
          # reduce lr, maybe keep theta
          lr *=0.7
          # occasional larger random jump
          if rng.random()<0.05:
             theta = theta + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=mean; a=softmax(theta); cur=...
      if it - last_improve > 5000: maybe reheat lr = 0.15 etc.
      it += 1
```

Make weights with `argpartition`: If K>=m, uniform. If `b` contains nan? not.
```
def make_weights(b, K=32, mix_broad=0.25, C=8.0):
    m = b.size
    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    if maxb <= 0: return np.full(m,1/m)
    if K >= m:
       mask = np.ones(m,dtype=bool)
    else:
       idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       mask = np.zeros(m,dtype=bool); mask[idx]=True
    # active within tolerance
    tol = maxb * 0.02  # within 2%
    if tol > 0:
       active = b >= maxb - tol
       if active.any(): mask |= active
    w = mask.astype(np.float64)
    s = w.sum()
    if s == 0: w = np.ones(m); s=m
    w /= s
    if mix_broad > 0:
       tau = maxb / C
       z = (b - maxb)/tau
       z -= z.max()
       sw = np.exp(z)
       ss = sw.sum()
       if ss <= 0 or not finite: sw = w
       else: sw /= ss
       w = (1-mix_broad)*w + mix_broad*sw
       w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
C maybe 6; `tol` 0.02 max not 0.05 to avoid too broad. TopK K controls.

Coordinate polish: Since θ optimizer may get stuck near boundary, coordinate moves can improve exact max. Implement simple random two-coordinate transfer with small line search using exact score and b? We can do after main local.
```
def coordinate_polish(a0, time_budget, rng):
   a=normalize(a0); best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
   n=a.size
   scale=0.05
   t0=time
   while time<t0+time_budget:
      improved=False
      for _ in range(200):
         i = rng.integers(n); j=rng.integers(n)
         if i==j: continue
         # move fraction frac of a[i] to j, with frac small random
         frac = scale * (0.2 + 0.8*rng.random())
         amt = frac * a[i]
         a_try = a.copy(); a_try[i]-=amt; a_try[j]+=amt
         if a_try[i] < 0: amt = a[i]*0.9; a_try[i]=0; a_try[j]+=amt
         # normalize to sum1 (copy slightly)
         a_try /= a_try.sum()
         sc=score(a_try)
         if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a_try.copy(); a=a_try.copy(); improved=True
         elif sc < score(a)? maybe accept if better current
             a=a_try.copy()
      if not improved:
         scale*=0.8
         if scale<1e-4: break
   return best_a,best_sc
```
But copying full array 200* many could be heavy. Limit to short. Use in-place careful.
Instead full random perturbations maybe simpler: `a_try = a + scale*rng.standard_normal(n); project_simplex` (previous). But O(n) copy. OK for short.
We can use coordinate polish after final best only, with time maybe 60s.

```
def init_power(n, gamma, alpha, reverse=False, floor=1e-6):
   i=np.arange(n)+alpha
   if reverse: i=i[::-1]
   a=i**(-gamma)
   a += floor  # maybe
   return normalize(a)
```
For gamma>1? Use 0.5-0.8. If gamma=0.8, a0 large; okay.

Implement `upsample_seq(a,m)`: use cumulative interpolation to target n:
```
def upsample_seq(a,n):
    old=a; s=old.size
    if s==n: return normalize(a)
    x_old=np.arange(s); y=old/old.sum()
    # cumulative: C_old at i+0.5? For each new j, target fraction (j+0.5)/n; find old fraction. Use inverse of cumulative density.
    cum=np.cumsum(y); cum=np.concatenate(([0],cum))
    x_new = np.interp((np.arange(n)+0.5)/n, cum, np.arange(s))
    out = np.interp(np.arange(n), x_new, y) # not preserving sum maybe; normalize
```
More directly, take quantile mapping: `u=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n`; `pos=np.interp(u, cum, np.arange(s)+0.5)`; out=np.interp(pos, np.arange(s)+0.5, y); normalize. This preserves shape area.

```
m=b.size
maxb=b.max()
# top K deterministic
if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
# active within 2%
active = np.where(b >= maxb*0.98)[0]
if active.size > 0:
   mask=np.zeros(m,bool); mask[idx]=True; mask[active]=True
   return np.nonzero(mask)[0]
else return idx
```

This adds O(m) to find cand and random choice. For n=2048,m=4095, O(m) okay. Random subset reduces bias and limits loops.
```
def select_top_indices(b,K,flat_tol=0.05, rng=None):
   m=b.size; maxb=b.max()
   if maxb <=0 or K<=0: ...
   cand = np.where(b >= maxb*(1-flat_tol))[0]
   if cand.size <= K:
       # ensure at least K with topK if cand small
       if cand.size < K:
          top = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
          mask=np.zeros(m,bool); mask[cand]=True; mask[top]=True
          return np.nonzero(mask)[0]
       return cand
   # random sample K from cand
   if rng is not None and cand.size > K:
       return rng.choice(cand, size=K, replace=False)
   else:
       # deterministic: take every stride
       step=cand.size//K
       idx = cand[::step][:K]
       if len(idx)<K: idx=np.pad? ; simpler top K
       return idx
```

Let's write `optimize_theta` with direct selection:
```
def optimize_theta(a0, time_budget, rng, lr0=None, K_factor=0.05, K_min=16, K_max=128, b1=0.9,b2=0.99, restart=2000, noise=0.08):
   n=a0.size
   a=normalize(a0.copy())
   theta=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=normed_score(a); cur_sc=best_sc
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   lr=lr0 or 0.15
   K=int(min(K_max, max(K_min, int(K_factor*(2*n-1)))))
   last_improve=0; no_improve=0
   it=0
   t_start=time.monotonic()
   while time.monotonic()-t_start < time_budget:
       b=full_conv(a)
       maxb=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0
       cur=2.0*n*maxb
       if cur < best_sc:
           best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if no_improve > restart:
           # reset
           theta=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
           theta += noise*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=theta.mean()
           a=softmax(theta); cur=normed_score(a)
           m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
           lr=0.08 + 0.12*rng.random()
           no_improve=0; last_improve=it
           it += 1
           continue
       idx=select_top_indices(b,K,0.05,rng)
       ga=grad_selected(a,idx)
       # Optional: if ga all zero? no.
       dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
       g=a*(ga-dot); g-=g.mean()
       # if g norm zero, perturb
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-14:
           g = 1e-6*rng.standard_normal(n); g-=g.mean()
       t += 1
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
       mh = m/(1-b1**t); vh = v/(1-b2**t)
       step = mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-12)
       # line search
       accepted=False
       best_trial=None
       for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
           th = theta - lr*fac*step
           th -= th.mean()
           # clip large? 
           atr=softmax(th)
           sc=normed_score(atr)
           if best_trial is None or sc < best_trial[0]: best_trial=(sc,th,atr)
           if sc <= cur*(1+1e-12):
               theta=th; a=atr; cur=sc; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # if best_trial better than previous but not better than cur? not possible because cur was old; maybe if it lowered from old but >cur? no
           # accept if best_trial significantly better than previous best? no
           if rng.random() < 0.02 and best_trial is not None and best_trial[0] < cur*(1+0.05):
               theta=best_trial[1]; a=best_trial[2]; cur=best_trial[0]
           else:
               lr *= 0.85
               if lr < 0.005:
                   # random kick
                   theta += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=theta.mean(); a=softmax(theta); cur=normed_score(a)
                   m=v=...
       if it - last_improve > 10000:
           lr = 0.08 +0.12*rng.random()
       it += 1
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Issue: We update m/v before line search; if accepted with factor <1, m/v based on full step not matched; okay. If rejected, m/v remains. Could improve by not updating m/v if rejected? But then t unused. Keep simple.

Select top indices: If using rng.choice on cand each iter for flat, may be expensive but fine. Need ensure K not >m. Use `np.argpartition` if no cand.
```
def select_top_indices(b,K,flat_frac,rng):
    m=b.size; maxb=float(np.max(b))
    if maxb <=0 or K<=0: return np.arange(m)
    cand = np.where(b >= maxb*(1-flat_frac))[0]
    if cand.size ==0: # fallback
       if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
       return np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    if cand.size <= K:
       if cand.size >= K: return cand
       # take top K overall to fill
       top = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       mask=np.zeros(m,bool); mask[cand]=True; mask[top]=True
       return np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    if rng is not None:
       # sample K without replacement using random integers
       perm = rng.permutation(cand.size)[:K]
       return cand[perm]
    else:
       step = cand.size // K
       idx = cand[::step]
       if idx.size < K: idx = np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]
       return idx
```
`rng.permutation(cand.size)` for cand size maybe thousands every iter could be expensive. Use `rng.choice(cand, size=K, replace=False)` simpler. It may copy. For speed, if cand.size > 4*K, use deterministic stride but rotate offset? To randomize cheaply, keep state offset: In optimize_theta, maintain `select_offset` increment; if flat, choose stride. if cand.size > K:
   # stride sampling with random start once per call
   if rng is not None:
       start = int(rng.integers(cand.size))
   else: start=0
   idx = []
   for cnt in range(K):
       idx.append(cand[(start + cnt*(cand.size//max(K,1))) % cand.size])
   # convert to array
```

return cand[pos]
```

```
def init_cosine_base(n,rng):
   gamma=0.35+0.3*rng.random()
   alpha=0.5+1.5*rng.random()
   i=np.arange(n)+alpha
   base=i**(-gamma)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   mod=np.ones(n)
   nterms=int(rng.integers(2,6))
   amp=0.2+0.3*rng.random()
   for term in range(nterms):
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,11))
       phase=2*np.pi*rng.random()
       mod += (amp/(term+1))*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+phase)
   mod = np.clip(mod,0.05,3.0)
   a=base*mod
   return normalize(a)
```

Now, let's sketch final code in detail.

Top imports: `import numpy as np, time, math` (scipy not required). Could import sys? no.

Define constants? no.

`next_pow2(L)`:
```
def _pow2(atleast):
    if atleast <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (atleast - 1).bit_length()
```

`full_conv(x)`:
```
def full_conv(x):
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],dtype=float)
    L=2*n-1
    if n < 32:
       y=np.convolve(x,x)
       return np.maximum(y,0.0)
    size=_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(x,size)
    y=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:L]
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    return y
```
For n=1, L=1, size=1; rfft size1 works. But if size power of two 1? bit_length(0)=0 =>1. Good.
`irfft(fa*fa, size)` for size=1 returns length1.

`normed_score(a)`:
```
def normed_score(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    b=full_conv(a)
    if b.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(b))
```
Assumes sum1. If not, maybe normalize? In search we normalize. For safety, `s=a.sum(); if s<=0: inf; return 2n*maxb/(s*s)`. This adds cost. We can have `score_norm` assume sum1. Use `normed_score` for candidates normalized. At evaluation previous maybe normalized. Use `score_exact(a)` with sum for final? Not needed.

`normalize(a)`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(a.size,1/a.size)
    return a/s
```

`softmax(theta)`:
```
def softmax(theta):
    th=np.asarray(theta,float)
    th=th - np.max(th)
    z=np.exp(th)
    s=z.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       th=th - np.min(th)
       z=np.exp(th); s=z.sum()
       if s<=0: z=np.ones_like(th)
       else z/=s
    else: z/=s
    return z
```

`select_top_indices` as above.

`grad_selected(a, idx)`:
```
def grad_selected(a, idx):
   n=a.size; g=np.zeros(n)
   if idx.size==0: return g
   c=2.0/float(idx.size)
   for kk in idx:
       k=int(kk)
       if k < n:
           g[:k+1] += c * a[k::-1]
       else:
           d=k-n+1
           if d>=n: continue
           g[d:n] += c * a[n-1:k-n:-1]
   return g
```
Need check slice when stop = k-n. If k=2n-2, stop=n-2, start n-1, slice length1. If k= n, stop0 length n-1. Good.
If k huge due b index 2n-2 max. For n=1, b indices 0, k<n, g[:1]+=a[0::-1]. ok.

Potential performance: For each k, `a[k::-1]` returns view, `g[:k+1] += c*view` creates temp c*view length. Could optimize by `g[:k+1] += c * a[k::-1]` ok. For large K=200, n=512, fine.

`optimize_theta` parameters:
```
def optimize_theta(a0, time_budget, rng, lr0=None, K_factor=None, K_min=None, K_max=None, b1=0.9, b2=0.99, restart=None, noise=None, flat_frac=0.05):
   if time_budget <=0: return normalize(a0), np.inf
   n=a0.size
   a=normalize(a0)
   theta=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=normed_score(a); cur_sc=best_sc
   if lr0 is None: lr0 = 0.12 + 0.12*rng.random()
   if K_factor is None: K_factor = 0.05
   if K_min is None: K_min=16
   if K_max is None: K_max=128
   if restart is None: restart=2000
   if noise is None: noise=0.08
   K=int(max(K_min, min(K_max, int(K_factor*(2*n-1)))))
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   lr=lr0
   last_improve=0; no_improve=0; it=0
   stuck=0
   t0=time.monotonic()
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
       b=full_conv(a)
       if b.size==0: break
       maxb=float(np.max(b))
       cur=2.0*n*maxb
       if cur < best_sc:
          best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          stuck +=1
       if no_improve > restart:
          theta=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
          theta += noise*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=theta.mean()
          a=softmax(theta)
          m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0
          lr=0.08+0.15*rng.random()
          no_improve=0; last_improve=it; it+=1; continue
       idx=select_top_indices(b,K,flat_frac,rng)
       ga=grad_selected(a,idx)
       # if ga has all zeros? add uniform? 
       dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
       g=a*(ga-dot)
       g-=g.mean()
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-15:
           g=1e-6*rng.standard_normal(n); g-=g.mean()
       t +=1
       bias1=1-b1**t; bias2=1-b2**t
       m = b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       v = b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
       mh=m/bias1; vh=v/bias2
       step=mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-12)
       # If step norm extreme, scale
       snorm=float(np.linalg.norm(step))
       if not np.isfinite(snorm) or snorm<1e-12:
           step=0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); step-=step.mean()
       elif snorm > 10.0:
           step *= 10.0/snorm
       # line search
       accepted=False
       best_sc_trial=np.inf; best_th=None
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
           th = theta - lr*fac*step
           th -= th.mean()
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
              continue
           atr=softmax(th)
           sc=normed_score(atr)
           if sc < best_sc_trial:
              best_sc_trial=sc; best_th=th; best_atr=atr
           if sc <= cur*(1+1e-12):
              theta=th; a=atr; cur=sc; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           if best_th is not None and (rng.random()<0.03 and best_sc_trial < cur*(1+0.02)):
               theta=best_th; a=best_atr; cur=best_sc_trial
           else:
               # no improvement; shrink lr
               lr *= 0.85
               if lr < 0.004:
                   # kick
                   theta += 0.04*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=theta.mean(); a=softmax(theta); cur=normed_score(a)
                   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
                   lr=0.08+0.1*rng.random()
               stuck +=1
               if stuck > 800:
                   theta=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
                   theta += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=theta.mean(); a=softmax(theta); cur=normed_score(a)
                   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
                   lr=0.08+0.15*rng.random()
                   no_improve=0; last_improve=it; stuck=0
       else:
           # if improved best? already updated in loop? Actually best updated at loop start only; if accepted and cur<best_sc not yet? We'll update now to avoid waiting.
           if cur < best_sc:
               best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it; no_improve=0
           # if steady improve, maybe gently increase lr
           if it - last_improve < 100:
               lr = min(0.5, lr*1.001)
       it +=1
       # To limit huge loops, occasional print? no per iter.
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Need ensure variables best_atr defined if best_sc_trial inf. In accepted branch, if no finite th (unlikely), best_th None. Use flags.

Now, coordinate polish with simplex projection:
```
def project_simplex(v):
   n=v.size
   if n==0: return v
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cssv=np.cumsum(u)
   idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   cond=u*idx > (cssv-1.0)
   rho=np.where(cond)[0][-1]
   theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
   return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
Works if all finite. For large n, sort O(n log n). Use in polish only.

def polish_perturb(a0,time_budget,rng):
   a=normalize(a0); best_a=a; best_sc=normed_score(a)
   scale=0.02
   t0=time.monotonic()
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget and scale>1e-6:
       improved=False
       # number of trials adapt to n: max(50, min(1000,int(100000/n)))
       n=a.size
       trials=max(30, min(500, int(200000/n)))
       t_iter=time.time? use local start.
       trials_t=min(time_budget-(now-t0), maybe)
       for _ in range(trials):
           if time>deadline break
           cand=a + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
           cand=project_simplex(cand)
           sc=normed_score(cand)
           if sc < best_sc:
               best_sc=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); improved=True
       if not improved:
           scale*=0.75
       else:
           scale=max(scale*0.9,1e-6)
   return best_a,best_sc

def get_inits(n,rng,prev=None):
   inits=[]
   if prev is not None and prev.size==n:
      inits.append(prev.copy())
      inits.append(prev[::-1].copy())
   # smooth functions
   inits.append(init_power(n,0.5,0.8,False))
   inits.append(init_power(n,0.5,1.0,True))
   inits.append(init_power(n,0.4,1.0,False))
   inits.append(init_power(n,0.6,1.5,False))
   inits.append(init_beta_two(n,0.3,0.3,1.0))
   inits.append(init_cosine_base(n,rng))
   inits.append(init_mixed(n,rng))
   # random
   a=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.2; inits.append(normalize(a))
   # if prev size different, resize one
   if prev is not None and prev.size != n and prev.size >=8:
       inits.append(resize_seq(prev,n))
   # ensure unique? not needed
   return inits
```

`resize_seq(a,n)` as quantile interpolation:
```
def resize_seq(a,n):
   a=normalize(a); m=a.size
   if m==n: return a
   cum=np.cumsum(a); cum=cum/cum[-1]
   cum=np.concatenate(([0.0], cum))
   u=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   x=np.interp(u, cum, np.arange(m)+0.5)
   out=np.interp(x, np.arange(m)+0.5, a)
   return normalize(out)
```
This maps new uniform u to old position; area preserving? Should integrate to 1. OK.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=2400):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(1.0,float(budget_s)-12.0)
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    prev=None
    try:
       h=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float)
       if h.ndim!=1 or h.size<4: raise ValueError
       h=np.clip(h,0,1000); h=normalize(h)
       sc=normed_score(h)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          prev=h; best_a=h.copy(); best_sc=sc
          print(f"[prev] n={h.size} score={sc:.6f}")
    except Exception:
       pass
    # n list
    n_list=[64,128,256,512,1024,2048]
    if best_a is not None:
       nb=best_a.size
       if nb not in n_list and 16<=nb<=4096:
           n_list.append(nb)
    n_list=sorted(set(n_list))
    # weights base
    if 64 in n_list: n_list.remove? Let's build weights dict.
    weights={}
    for nv in n_list:
        if nv<=64: weights[nv]=0.04
        elif nv<=128: weights[nv]=0.25
        elif nv<=256: weights[nv]=0.25
        elif nv<=512: weights[nv]=0.20
        elif nv<=1024: weights[nv]=0.15
        else: weights[nv]=0.08
    # boost prev n
    if best_a is not None:
       weights[best_a.size]=weights.get(best_a.size,0.05)+0.12
    total_w=sum(weights.values())
    # total budget for phases: leave 20% final
    total_time=deadline-time.monotonic()
    phase_total=total_time*0.78
    # Prepare phase order: maybe better n first (medium). Sort by -weight? or n ascending? We want early improvements. Sort by n ascending? 128,256,512... If prev n, maybe first. Let's sort by weight desc then n.
    order=sorted(n_list, key=lambda x:(-weights[x], x))
    for nv in order:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       phase_time=weights[nv]/total_w*phase_total
       if phase_time < 10: continue
       inits=get_inits(nv,rng,prev)
       # decide how many inits can fit
       max_inits=max(1,int(phase_time//15))  # at least 15s per init
       if len(inits)>max_inits: inits=inits[:max_inits]
       per_time=phase_time/len(inits)
       for ai,a_init in enumerate(inits):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          tb=min(per_time, deadline-time.monotonic()-2.0)
          if tb<5: break
          # random hyperparams
          lr0=0.08+0.15*rng.random()
          K_factor=0.04+0.04*rng.random()
          a_best,sc_best=optimize_theta(a_init,tb,rng,lr0=lr0,K_factor=K_factor)
          if sc_best < best_sc:
              best_sc=sc_best; best_a=a_best.copy()
              print(f"[run] n={nv} init={ai} score={sc_best:.6f} BEST")
          else:
              print(f"[run] n={nv} init={ai} score={sc_best:.6f}")
       print(f"[phase] n={nv} done best={best_sc:.6f} time_left={deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}")
    # final polish
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.ones(128)/128; best_sc=2.0
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-5.0
    if remaining > 20:
       # polish best a with relax
       tb1=min(remaining*0.55, 300.0)
       a1,sc1=optimize_theta(best_a,tb1,rng,lr0=0.05,K_factor=0.02)
       if sc1<best_sc: best_sc=sc1; best_a=a1.copy(); print
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-5.0
       if remaining > 15:
          tb2=min(remaining*0.4, 120.0)
          a2,sc2=optimize_theta(best_a,tb2,rng,lr0=0.08,K_factor=0.08)
          update
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-4.0
       if remaining > 10:
          tb3=min(remaining, 60.0)
          a3,sc3=polish_perturb(best_a,tb3,rng)
          update
    # Final evaluate candidates: best and prev maybe
    candidates=[]
    if best_a is not None: candidates.append(best_a)
    if prev is not None and (best_a is None or prev.size != best_a.size or not np.array_equal(prev,best_a)): candidates.append(prev)
    # maybe uniform fallback
    best_out=best_a
    best_final=np.inf
    for cand in candidates:
       lst=[float(x) for x in cand]
       try:
          ev=evaluate_sequence(lst)
       except Exception:
          ev=np.inf
       if ev < best_final:
          best_final=ev; best_out=cand
    if not np.isfinite(best_final) or best_out is None:
       best_out=np.ones(256)/256
    final_lst=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in best_out]
    # final evaluator
    try: print(f"[final] n={len(final_lst)} score={evaluate_sequence(final_lst):.6f}")
    except: pass
    return final_lst
```

Detailed functions:

```
def _pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`full_conv`:
```
def full_conv(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    L = 2*n - 1
    if n <= 32:
        y = np.convolve(x, x, mode='full')
    else:
        size = _pow2(L)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(x, size)
        y = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:L]
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return y
```
For n=1, L=1, size=1, rfft ok. `irfft` with size=1 expects fa length1, returns length1. Good.

`normed_score`:
```
def normed_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
    b=full_conv(a)
    if b.size ==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*float(np.max(b))/(s*s))
```
Even if sum not1. Good slightly cost. In optimize all normalized; sum cost negligible. Use this everywhere.

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       if a.size==0: return a
       return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
    return a/s
```

`softmax`: as above.

`project_simplex`: Need if sum v >? works for any finite. If v large, `cssv-1` etc. Use float.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    mask = u * idx > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not mask.any():
        return np.ones(n)/n
    rho = np.where(mask)[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```

`select_top_indices`:
```
def select_top_indices(b, K, flat_frac, rng):
    m = b.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    if maxb <= 0 or K <= 0:
        return np.arange(m)
    if K >= m:
        return np.arange(m)
    threshold = maxb * (1.0 - flat_frac)
    cand = np.where(b >= threshold)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        return np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    if cand.size <= K:
        if cand.size == m or cand.size >= K:
            return cand
        top = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
        mask[np.concatenate((cand, top))] = True
        return np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    # sample K from cand with stride
    if rng is not None:
        start = int(rng.integers(cand.size))
    else:
        start = 0
    step = cand.size // K
    if step <= 0:
        step = 1
    pos = (start + np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64) * step) % cand.size
    # ensure unique if possible by fallback
    idx = cand[pos]
    if idx.size != K or len(np.unique(idx)) < K:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    return idx
```
`np.unique` for K up to256 small. Good.
Potential if cand.size large and step=1, pos unique if start+range within mod? If start>0 and step=1, pos wraps, still unique if K<=cand.size. Good.
If cand.size=100,K=64, step=1, unique. If step>1, gcd could cause duplicates if K > cycle length. We check unique.

`grad_selected` as above. Need if slice stop negative? For k>=n, stop=k-n >=0. Good. If k==n-1 first branch. For n=1, k=0 branch.

`init_power`: If alpha maybe 0? ensure >0.
```
def init_power(n,gamma,alpha,reverse=False):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+max(0.1,alpha)
   if reverse: i=i[::-1]
   a=np.power(i, -gamma)
   a += 1e-12
   return normalize(a)
```
If gamma negative? not.

`init_beta_two`:
```
def init_beta_two(n, gl, gr, alpha=1.0):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+max(0.1,alpha)
   j=float(n)-np.arange(n)+max(0.1,alpha)
   a=np.power(i, -gl)*np.power(j, -gr)
   return normalize(a)
```
If gr=0, power 0 =1.

`init_cosine_base`:
```
def init_cosine_base(n,rng):
   gamma=0.35+0.35*rng.random()
   alpha=0.5+1.5*rng.random()
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+alpha
   base=np.power(i, -gamma)
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   mod=np.ones(n)
   nterms=int(rng.integers(2,6))
   amp=0.25+0.35*rng.random()
   for tnum in range(nterms):
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,12))
       phase=2.0*np.pi*rng.random()
       mod += (amp/(float(tnum)+1.0))*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*freq*x+phase)
   mod=np.clip(mod,0.05,3.0)
   a=base*mod
   a += 1e-12
   return normalize(a)
```
`init_mixed`:
```
def init_mixed(n,rng):
   gamma=0.35+0.3*rng.random()
   alpha=0.5+1.5*rng.random()
   i=np.arange(n)+alpha
   base=np.power(i,-gamma)
   base=base/base.sum()
   center=float(rng.uniform(0.25,0.75))
   width=float(rng.uniform(0.08,0.35))
   x=np.arange(n)/max(n-1,1)
   bump=np.exp(-((x-center)/width)**2)
   bump=bump/bump.sum()
   mixp=float(rng.uniform(0.55,0.95))
   a=mixp*base+(1-mixp)*bump
   return normalize(a)
```

`resize_seq` as above with nan_guard.
```
def resize_seq(a,n):
    a=normalize(a); m=a.size
    if m==n: return a
    cum=np.cumsum(a)/a.sum()
    cum=np.concatenate(([0.0], cum))
    u=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    xold=np.interp(u, cum, np.arange(m)+0.5)
    out=np.interp(np.arange(n), xold, a) # Wait mapping? We want out j value at old cumulative u_j? We used xold as old position for each new j. Then out_j = a interpolated at xold_j. This gives density at old position for new equal spacing; sum may not 1. normalize.
    return normalize(out)
```
This is rough but okay.

`get_inits`:
```
def get_inits(n,rng,prev):
    inits=[]
    if prev is not None:
       if prev.size == n:
          inits.append(prev.copy())
          inits.append(prev[::-1].copy())
       elif prev.size >= 8:
          inits.append(resize_seq(prev,n))
    inits.append(init_power(n,0.5,0.8,False))
    inits.append(init_power(n,0.5,1.0,True))
    inits.append(init_power(n,0.4,1.0,False))
    inits.append(init_power(n,0.6,1.5,False))
    inits.append(init_beta_two(n,0.3,0.3,1.0))
    inits.append(init_cosine_base(n,rng))
    inits.append(init_mixed(n,rng))
    # random smooth/noisy
    a=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.2
    inits.append(normalize(a))
    # random power reverse
    if n>1:
       inits.append(init_power(n,0.45,0.8,rng.random()<0.5))
    return inits
```
If prev resized plus prev same may duplicate; not issue.

`polish_perturb` with local time:
```
def polish_perturb(a0,time_budget,rng):
    a=normalize(a0); n=a.size
    if n==0: return a,np.inf
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=normed_score(a)
    scale=0.02
    t0=time.monotonic()
    limit=t0+max(0.5,time_budget)
    while scale > 1e-5 and time.monotonic() < limit:
        improved=False
        if n <= 512:
            trials=300
        elif n <= 2048:
            trials=80
        else:
            trials=40
        trial_start=time.monotonic()
        for _ in range(trials):
            if time.monotonic() > limit: break
            cand = a + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
            cand = project_simplex(cand)
            sc = normed_score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); improved=True
        if not improved:
            scale *= 0.7
        else:
            scale = max(scale*0.85, 1e-6)
        # prevent too long per scale if n large
        if time.monotonic() > limit: break
    return best_a,best_sc
```

def random_mask_search(base, trials, rng, scale=0.5):
   a0=normalize(base)
   best=a0; best_sc=normed_score(a0)
   for _ in range(trials):
      mask = 1.0 + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
      mask=np.clip(mask,0.1,3.0)
      cand=normalize(a0*mask)
      sc=normed_score(cand)
      if sc<best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
   return best,best_sc
```

Let's write `optimize_theta` fully with try/except for numerical issues:
```
def optimize_theta(a0, time_budget, rng, lr0=None, K_factor=None, K_min=None, K_max=None, b1=0.9, b2=0.99, restart=None, noise=None, flat_frac=0.05):
    if time_budget is None or time_budget <= 0:
        a=normalize(a0); return a,np.inf
    n = a0.size
    a = normalize(a0)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)); theta -= theta.mean()
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = normed_score(a); cur_sc = best_sc
    # params defaults
    if lr0 is None: lr0 = 0.08 + 0.15*float(rng.random())
    if K_factor is None: K_factor = 0.10
    if K_min is None: K_min = 64
    if K_max is None: K_max = 256
    if restart is None: restart = 2500
    if noise is None: noise = 0.07
    K = int(max(K_min, min(K_max, int(K_factor * (2*n - 1)))))
    if K < 1: K=1
    mom = np.zeros(n); var=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr = float(lr0)
    no_improve=0; rejects=0; it=0; last_improve=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); limit=t0+float(time_budget)
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
       b=full_conv(a)
       if b.size ==0: break
       maxb=float(np.max(b))
       cur=2.0*n*maxb
       if cur < best_sc:
          best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve += 1
       if not np.isfinite(cur) or no_improve > restart:
          theta = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300)); theta -= theta.mean()
          theta += noise * rng.standard_normal(n); theta -= theta.mean()
          a = softmax(theta)
          mom.fill(0.0); var.fill(0.0); t=0
          lr = 0.08 + 0.15*float(rng.random())
          no_improve=0; rejects=0; last_improve=it; it+=1
          continue
       idx = select_top_indices(b, K, flat_frac, rng)
       ga = grad_selected(a, idx)
       dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
       g = a * (ga - dot); g -= g.mean()
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
          g = 0.0
       gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-15:
          g = 1e-6 * rng.standard_normal(n); g -= g.mean(); gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-15:
          # reset
          ...
       t += 1
       bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
       bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
       mom = b1*mom + (1.0-b1)*g
       var = b2*var + (1.0-b2)*(g*g)
       step = (mom / bias1) / (np.sqrt(var / bias2) + 1e-12)
       snorm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
       if not np.isfinite(snorm) or snorm < 1e-12:
           step = 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n); step -= step.mean(); snorm = float(np.linalg.norm(step)); if snorm<1e-12: step=np.zeros(n)
       elif snorm > 8.0:
           step *= 8.0 / snorm
       accepted = False
       best_trial_sc = np.inf; best_trial_theta=None; best_trial_a=None
       for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
           th = theta - lr*fac*step
           th -= th.mean()
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
              continue
           atr = softmax(th)
           sc = normed_score(atr)
           if sc < best_trial_sc:
               best_trial_sc=sc; best_trial_theta=th; best_trial_a=atr
           if sc <= cur*(1.0+1e-12):
               theta=th; a=atr; cur=sc; accepted=True
               break
       if accepted:
           if cur < best_sc:
               best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it; no_improve=0
           rejects=0
           if lr < 0.4:
               lr = min(0.5, lr*1.001)
       else:
           rejects += 1
           if best_trial_theta is not None and (rng.random() < 0.04 and best_trial_sc < cur*(1.0+0.05)):
               theta = best_trial_theta; a = best_trial_a; cur = best_trial_sc
               if cur < best_sc: ...
               rejects=0
           else:
               lr *= 0.85
               if lr < 0.004:
                   theta += 0.03*rng.standard_normal(n); theta -= theta.mean(); a=softmax(theta); cur=normed_score(a)
                   mom.fill(0.0); var.fill(0.0); t=0; lr=0.08+0.1*rng.random()
               if rejects > 250:
                   theta = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
                   theta += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); theta-=theta.mean(); a=softmax(theta); cur=normed_score(a)
                   mom.fill(0.0); var.fill(0.0); t=0
                   lr=0.08+0.15*rng.random()
                   no_improve=0; last_improve=it; rejects=0
       it += 1
    # optionally ensure best is finite
    return best_a,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The algorithm combines:
  - FFT-based exact convolution/score evaluations,
  - a direct top-index subgradient for the active convolution coefficients,
  - Adam-style log-space optimization on the softmax parameterization,
  - line search, annealing-style random accepts, restarts, and local polish,
  - many diverse analytic/random initializations and multiple sequence lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        if a.size == 0:
            return a
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size)
    return a / s


def normed_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float(np.inf)
    b = full_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float(np.inf)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b)))


def normed_score1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float(np.inf)
    b = full_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float(np.inf)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b)))


def full_conv(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 32:
        y = np.convolve(x, x, mode="full")
    else:
        size = _pow2(L)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(x, size)
        y = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:L]

    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return y


def softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=float)
    theta = theta - np.max(theta)
    z = np.exp(theta)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        theta = theta - np.min(theta)
        z = np.exp(theta)
        s = float(z.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.ones_like(theta) / theta.size
        return z / s
    return z / s


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    mask = u * idx > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not mask.any():
        return np.ones(n) / n
    rho = int(np.where(mask)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def select_top_indices(b, K, flat_frac, rng):
    m = b.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int)

    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    if maxb <= 0.0 or K <= 0:
        return np.arange(m)

    if K >= m:
        return np.arange(m)

    threshold = maxb * (1.0 - flat_frac)
    cand = np.where(b >= threshold)[0]

    if cand.size == 0:
        return np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]

    if cand.size <= K:
        if cand.size >= K:
            return cand
        top = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
        mask[cand] = True
        mask[top] = True
        return np.nonzero(mask)[0]

    if rng is not None:
        start = int(rng.integers(cand.size))
    else:
        start = 0

    step = cand.size // K
    if step <= 0:
        step = 1
    pos = (start + np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64) * step) % cand.size
    idx = cand[pos]

    if idx.size != K or len(np.unique(idx)) < K:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    return idx


def grad_selected(a, idx):
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return g

    c = 2.0 / float(idx.size)
    for kk in idx:
        k = int(kk)
        if k < 0 or k >= 2 * n - 1:
            continue
        if k < n:
            g[:k + 1] += c * a[k::-1]
        else:
            d = k - n + 1
            if d >= n:
                continue
            g[d:n] += c * a[n - 1:k - n:-1]
    return g


def init_power(n, gamma, alpha, reverse=False):
    alpha = max(0.1, float(alpha))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + alpha
    if reverse:
        i = i[::-1]
    a = np.power(i, -float(gamma))
    a += 1e-12
    return normalize(a)


def init_beta_two(n, gl, gr, alpha=1.0):
    alpha = max(0.1, float(alpha))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + alpha
    j = float(n) - np.arange(n) + alpha
    a = np.power(i, -float(gl)) * np.power(j, -float(gr))
    a += 1e-12
    return normalize(a)


def init_cosine_base(n, rng):
    gamma = 0.35 + 0.35 * float(rng.random())
    alpha = 0.5 + 1.5 * float(rng.random())
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + alpha
    base = np.power(i, -gamma)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    mod = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    terms = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
    amp = 0.25 + 0.35 * float(rng.random())

    for t in range(terms):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 12))
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        mod += (amp / float(t + 1)) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)

    mod = np.clip(mod, 0.05, 3.0)
    a = base * mod
    a += 1e-12
    return normalize(a)


def init_mixed(n, rng):
    gamma = 0.35 + 0.30 * float(rng.random())
    alpha = 0.5 + 1.5 * float(rng.random())
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + alpha
    base = np.power(i, -gamma)
    base = base / base.sum()

    center = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
    width = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / max(n - 1, 1)
    bump = np.exp(-((x - center) / width) ** 2)
    bump = bump / bump.sum()

    mixp = float(rng.uniform(0.55, 0.95))
    a = mixp * base + (1.0 - mixp) * bump
    return normalize(a)


def resize_seq(a, n):
    a = normalize(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return a

    cum = np.cumsum(a)
    cum = cum / float(cum[-1])
    cum = np.concatenate(([0.0], cum))

    u = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    old_pos = np.interp(u, cum, np.arange(m) + 0.5)
    out = np.interp(np.arange(n, dtype=float), old_pos, a)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return normalize(out)


def get_inits(n, rng, prev):
    inits = []

    if prev is not None:
        if prev.size == n:
            inits.append(prev.copy())
            inits.append(prev[::-1].copy())
        elif prev.size >= 8:
            inits.append(resize_seq(prev, n))

    inits.append(init_power(n, 0.50, 0.80, False))
    inits.append(init_power(n, 0.50, 1.00, True))
    inits.append(init_power(n, 0.40, 1.00, False))
    inits.append(init_power(n, 0.60, 1.50, False))
    inits.append(init_beta_two(n, 0.30, 0.30, 1.0))
    inits.append(init_cosine_base(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_mixed(n, rng))

    a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.2
    inits.append(normalize(a))

    if n > 1:
        inits.append(init_power(n, 0.45, 0.80, bool(rng.random() < 0.5)))

    return inits


def optimize_theta(a0, time_budget, rng,
                   lr0=None, K_factor=None, K_min=None, K_max=None,
                   b1=0.9, b2=0.99, restart=None, noise=None, flat_frac=0.05):
    if time_budget is None or time_budget <= 0.0:
        a = normalize(a0)
        return a, normed_score1(a)

    n = int(np.asarray(a0).size)
    a = normalize(a0)

    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    theta -= float(theta.mean())

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = normed_score1(a)
    cur_sc = best_sc

    if lr0 is None:
        lr0 = 0.08 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
    if K_factor is None:
        K_factor = 0.10
    if K_min is None:
        K_min = 64
    if K_max is None:
        K_max = 256
    if restart is None:
        restart = 2500
    if noise is None:
        noise = 0.07

    K = int(max(K_min, min(K_max, int(K_factor * (2 * n - 1)))))
    if K < 1:
        K = 1

    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    var = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    t = 0
    lr = float(lr0)

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    rejects = 0
    last_improve = 0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = t0 + float(time_budget)

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        b = full_conv(a)
        if b.size == 0:
            break

        maxb = float(np.max(b))
        cur = 2.0 * n * maxb

        if cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_improve = it
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if not np.isfinite(cur) or no_improve > restart:
            theta = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
            theta -= float(theta.mean())
            theta += noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
            theta -= float(theta.mean())
            a = softmax(theta)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            var.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.08 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
            no_improve = 0
            rejects = 0
            last_improve = it
            it += 1
            continue

        idx = select_top_indices(b, K, flat_frac, rng)
        ga = grad_selected(a, idx)
        dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
        g = a * (ga - dot)
        g -= float(g.mean())

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.zeros_like(g)

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            g = 1e-6 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            g -= float(g.mean())
            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))

        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            theta = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
            theta -= float(theta.mean())
            theta += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            theta -= float(theta.mean())
            a = softmax(theta)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            var.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.08 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
            no_improve = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        t += 1
        bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
        bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t

        mom = b1 * mom + (1.0 - b1) * g
        var = b2 * var + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)

        step = (mom / bias1) / (np.sqrt(var / bias2) + 1e-12)

        snorm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
        if not np.isfinite(snorm) or snorm < 1e-12:
            step = 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            step -= float(step.mean())
            snorm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
            if snorm < 1e-12:
                step = rng.standard_normal(n)
                step -= float(step.mean())
        elif snorm > 8.0:
            step *= 8.0 / snorm

        accepted = False
        best_trial_sc = float(np.inf)
        best_trial_theta = None
        best_trial_a = None

        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            th = theta - lr * fac * step
            th -= float(th.mean())
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                continue

            atr = softmax(th)
            sc = normed_score1(atr)

            if sc < best_trial_sc:
                best_trial_sc = sc
                best_trial_theta = th
                best_trial_a = atr

            if sc <= cur * (1.0 + 1e-12):
                theta = th
                a = atr
                cur = sc
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if cur < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_improve = it
                no_improve = 0
            rejects = 0
            if lr < 0.4:
                lr = min(0.5, lr * 1.001)
        else:
            rejects += 1
            if (best_trial_theta is not None and best_trial_a is not None and
                    best_trial_sc < float(np.inf) and
                    rng.random() < 0.04 and
                    best_trial_sc < cur * 1.05):
                theta = best_trial_theta
                a = best_trial_a
                cur = best_trial_sc
                if cur < best_sc:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    last_improve = it
                    no_improve = 0
                rejects = 0
            else:
                lr *= 0.85
                if lr < 0.004:
                    theta += 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    theta -= float(theta.mean())
                    a = softmax(theta)
                    cur = normed_score1(a)
                    mom.fill(0.0)
                    var.fill(0.0)
                    t = 0
                    lr = 0.08 + 0.10 * float(rng.random())

                if rejects > 250:
                    theta = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
                    theta -= float(theta.mean())
                    theta += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    theta -= float(theta.mean())
                    a = softmax(theta)
                    cur = normed_score1(a)
                    mom.fill(0.0)
                    var.fill(0.0)
                    t = 0
                    lr = 0.08 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
                    no_improve = 0
                    last_improve = it
                    rejects = 0

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def polish_perturb(a0, time_budget, rng):
    if time_budget is None or time_budget <= 0.0:
        a = normalize(a0)
        return a, normed_score1(a)

    a = normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float(np.inf)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = normed_score1(a)

    scale = 0.02
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = t0 + float(time_budget)

    while scale > 1e-6 and time.monotonic() < limit:
        improved = False

        if n <= 512:
            trials = 300
        elif n <= 2048:
            trials = 80
        else:
            trials = 40

        for _ in range(trials):
            if time.monotonic() >= limit:
                break
            cand = a + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
            cand = project_simplex(cand)
            sc = normed_score1(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
                a = cand.copy()
                improved = True

        if improved:
            scale = max(scale * 0.85, 1e-6)
        else:
            scale *= 0.7

    return best_a, best_sc


def min_time_for_len(n):
    if n <= 256:
        return 12.0
    if n <= 512:
        return 20.0
    if n <= 1024:
        return 40.0
    return 70.0


def phase_weights(n_list):
    weights = {}
    for nv in n_list:
        if nv <= 128:
            weights[nv] = 0.26
        elif nv <= 256:
            weights[nv] = 0.28
        elif nv <= 512:
            weights[nv] = 0.20
        elif nv <= 1024:
            weights[nv] = 0.14
        else:
            weights[nv] = 0.12
    return weights


def safe_sequence_list(x):
    out = []
    for v in x:
        try:
            v = float(v)
        except Exception:
            v = 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            v = 0.0
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        if v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        out.append(v)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total_budget - 12.0)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float(np.inf)
    prev = None

    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        s = float(h.sum())
        if h.ndim == 1 and h.size >= 8 and s > 0.01 and np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            prev = h / s
            ps = normed_score1(prev)
            if np.isfinite(ps):
                best_a = prev.copy()
                best_sc = ps
                print(f"[init] previous sequence n={prev.size} score={ps:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    n_list = [128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048]
    if best_a is not None:
        nb = int(best_a.size)
        if 32 <= nb <= 4096 and nb not in n_list:
            n_list.append(nb)
    n_list = sorted(set(int(x) for x in n_list))

    if not n_list:
        n_list = [128]

    weights = phase_weights(n_list)
    if best_a is not None:
        nb = int(best_a.size)
        weights[nb] = weights.get(nb, 0.05) + 0.05

    order = []
    if best_a is not None and int(best_a.size) in n_list:
        order.append(int(best_a.size))
    for nv in sorted(n_list):
        if nv not in order:
            order.append(nv)

    total_time = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    phase_total = total_time * 0.78
    total_w = float(sum(weights.values()))

    for nv in order:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        w = float(weights.get(nv, 0.05))
        phase_time = w / total_w * phase_total
        if len(n_list) > 3:
            phase_time = min(phase_time, 0.34 * phase_total)
        if phase_time < 5.0:
            continue

        inits = get_inits(nv, rng, prev)
        min_per = min_time_for_len(nv)
        max_inits = max(1, int(phase_time // min_per))
        if len(inits) > max_inits:
            inits = inits[:max_inits]
        per_time = phase_time / max(1, len(inits))

        for ii, a_init in enumerate(inits):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0
            tb = min(per_time, rem)
            if tb < 4.0:
                break

            lr0 = 0.07 + 0.18 * float(rng.random())
            kf = 0.05 + 0.08 * float(rng.random())

            a_best, sc_best = optimize_theta(
                a_init,
                tb,
                rng,
                lr0=lr0,
                K_factor=kf
            )

            if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_best
                best_a = a_best.copy()
                print(f"[run] n={nv} init={ii} score={sc_best:.6f} BEST")
            else:
                print(f"[run] n={nv} init={ii} score={sc_best:.6f}")

        print(f"[phase] n={nv} done, current best={best_sc:.6f}, left={deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}s")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(128) / 128.0
        best_sc = 2.0

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
    if remaining > 25.0:
        tb1 = min(remaining * 0.50, 320.0)
        if tb1 > 10.0:
            a1, sc1 = optimize_theta(
                best_a, tb1, rng,
                lr0=0.04, K_factor=0.08, K_min=32, K_max=96
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc1
                best_a = a1.copy()
                print(f"[polish1] n={best_a.size} score={best_sc:.6f}")

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
        if remaining > 20.0:
            tb2 = min(remaining * 0.35, 180.0)
            if tb2 > 8.0:
                a2, sc2 = optimize_theta(
                    best_a, tb2, rng,
                    lr0=0.08, K_factor=0.12, K_min=64, K_max=160
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    print(f"[polish2] n={best_a.size} score={best_sc:.6f}")

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 4.0
        if remaining > 12.0:
            tb3 = min(remaining, 80.0)
            a3, sc3 = polish_perturb(best_a, tb3, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc3
                best_a = a3.copy()
                print(f"[polish3] n={best_a.size} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    candidates = []
    if best_a is not None:
        candidates.append(best_a)
    if prev is not None:
        candidates.append(prev)

    final_best = None
    final_best_score = float(np.inf)

    for cand in candidates:
        if cand is None or cand.size == 0:
            continue
        lst = safe_sequence_list(cand)
        try:
            ev = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            ev = float(np.inf)
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_best_score:
            final_best_score = ev
            final_best = lst

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = safe_sequence_list(np.ones(256) / 256.0)

    final_best = safe_sequence_list(final_best)
    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(final_best))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = float(np.inf)

    print(f"[final] n={len(final_best)} evaluator_score={final_eval:.6f}")
    return final_best
```
